Evaluations on STAGE-Eval, our source-grounded benchmark with an 851-example test set, show that STAGE produces stronger training data than existing approaches. This improves Qwen3-4B exact match from 31.37% to 74.27% and value accuracy from 45.46% to 90.69%.

</details>


### [142] [IHUBERT: Vector-Based Semantic Deduplication and Domain-Balanced Pretraining for Persian Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20089)
*Arash Ghafouri,Mahdi Firouzmandi,Hossein Saberi,Mohammad Reza Hasani Ahangar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IHUBERT 是一个从零训练的波斯语 RoBERTa-base 预训练语言模型，基于高质量去重语料和专用 BPE 分词器，在问答和自然语言推理任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有波斯语预训练语言模型受限于大规模高质量预训练语料不足，且评测多集中在分类和命名实体识别等标准任务，缺乏对阅读理解、关系抽取等更广泛 NLU 能力的系统评估。

Method: 作者使用 Sepahr-Danesh 集合中经筛选的 45GB 语料（约 70–80 亿 tokens），通过规范化、精确/近似重复删除、匿名化以及基于向量数据库的语义去重来提升语料质量与领域分布平衡；随后从零训练 125M 参数 RoBERTa-base 编码器，并在完整语料上训练 139k 词表的 BPE 分词器；最后在七个波斯语 NLU 基准上评测，包括 NER、情感分析、主题分类、NLI、抽取式问答和关系抽取。

Result: IHUBERT 在抽取式问答上取得最强提升，在 PQuAD 上 F1 为 88.3542，在 ParsiNLU-RC 上 F1 为 49.0987，均排名第一；在 FarsTail 自然语言推理任务上 Macro-F1 达 0.8350，为最佳结果；在 NER 和主题分类上保持竞争力，如 ParsTwiNER F1 为 0.8308、DigiMag Macro-F1 为 0.7953；但在关系抽取 PERLEX 上 Macro-F1 为 0.6684，仍是主要短板。分词器消融实验显示，在相同词表规模下 BPE 的子词碎片化略低于 WordPiece。

Conclusion: 该工作通过大规模语义清洗与去重的波斯语预训练语料、面向波斯语形态和拼写变化设计的 BPE 分词器，以及跨任务广泛评估，推进了波斯语语言建模研究；其优势主要体现在理解型任务，尤其是抽取式问答和自然语言推理，但关系抽取能力仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Persian pretrained language models (PLMs) are still limited by the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality pretraining corpora and by insufficient evaluation beyond standard classification and NER tasks. We present IHUBERT, a monolingual Persian PLM trained from scratch with the RoBERTa-base encoder (125M parameters) on a 45 GB curated subset of the Sepahr-Danesh collection (about 7-8B tokens). To improve corpus quality and reduce redundancy, we employ a multi-stage preprocessing pipeline that includes normalization, exact and near-duplicate removal, anonymization, and vector-database-based semantic deduplication for distribution balancing control across domains and registers. We additionally train a 139k-vocabulary BPE tokenizer on the full pretraining corpus to better capture Persian morphology and orthographic variation. IHUBERT is evaluated on seven Persian NLU benchmarks covering NER, sentiment analysis, topic classification, NLI, extractive question answering, and relation extraction, using task-standard metrics (entity-level F1, Macro-F1, EM/F1). IHUBERT achieves its strongest gains on extractive QA, ranking first on both PQuAD (F1 88.3542) and ParsiNLU-RC (F1 49.0987), and attains the best result on FarsTail (Macro-F1 0.8350). On NER and topic classification, it remains competitive (e.g., 0.8308 F1 on ParsTwiNER; 0.7953 Macro-F1 on DigiMag), while relation extraction remains the main remaining gap (0.6684 Macro-F1 on PERLEX). A controlled tokenizer ablation on the IHUBERT pretraining corpus shows that BPE yields slightly lower subword fragmentation than WordPiece at matched vocabulary size, supporting our tokenization design. Overall, IHUBERT advances Persian language modeling through semantically curated large-scale pretraining and broad evaluation across both classification and comprehension-oriented tasks.

</details>


### [143] [Self-Preference Is Weak or Absent in Verifiable Instruction-Following Revision: A Four-Model Test Under Genuine Authorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20093)
*William Guey,Pierrick Bougault*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究检验LLM在修改自身文本时是否会拒绝经确定性验证器确认的有效修正；在IFEval约束修订任务上未发现显著自我偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究显示LLM作为评审时可能偏好自身生成内容，因此需要检验它们是否也会抵触对自身写作的有效纠正，尤其是在纠正有效性由确定性检查器而非另一个模型判定的场景中。

Method: 模型先生成违反IFEval约束的草稿；官方IFEval checker确认草稿违规且候选编辑可修复违规；随后比较同一模型作为原始作者与作为新鲜中立评审者时，对该有效编辑的接受或拒绝情况。实验覆盖四个中等规模模型家族，共85组作者对比新鲜模型的比较。

Result: 未检测到自我偏好：作者拒绝已验证有效修正的比例与新鲜模型基本相同，差距为-5.1个百分点，95%置信区间为[-12.9,+2.7]。小规模先导实验中的自我怀疑趋势未能在大规模实验中复现。定性结果显示，作者拒绝有效修正时，97%的理由属于发现缺陷而非偏好表达。

Conclusion: 在该IFEval确定性验证设定下，LLM并不会显著抵触对自身文本的有效修正；若存在效应，也可能小于当前样本量可排除范围，约13个百分点以内的效应仍不能排除。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly review and revise text, including their own. A documented self-preference bias (models favoring their own generations when acting as judges) raises the question of whether models also resist valid corrections to their own writing. We test this in a setting where "valid" is decided not by another model but by a deterministic verifier: instruction-following revision on IFEval. A model writes a draft; the official IFEval checker confirms the draft violates a constraint and that a candidate edit fixes it; the model then accepts or rejects that edit either as the genuine in-context author or as a fresh model that sees the draft neutrally. Across four mid-tier model families and 85 author-versus-fresh comparisons, we find no detectable self-preference: authors reject verified-good fixes to their own drafts at essentially the same rate as fresh models judging the same drafts (gap -5.1 pp, 95% CI [-12.9, +2.7]). A self-skepticism hint from a smaller pilot did not replicate at scale. The one robust observation is qualitative: when authors do reject a verified-good fix, 97% of their stated reasons are flaw-catching rather than preference, that is, about the character of rejections, not an elevated rate. Effects smaller than ~13 pp cannot be excluded at this sample size.

</details>


### [144] [HydraHead: From Head-Level Functional Heterogeneity to Specialized Attention Hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20097)
*Zhentao Tan,Wei Chen,Jingyi Shen,Yao Liu,Xu Shen,Yue Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 HydraHead：一种沿注意力头维度融合全注意力与线性注意力的混合架构，在长上下文任务中以更低全注意力比例获得更强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全注意力的二次复杂度限制长上下文处理；现有开源混合注意力模型多采用按层混合，但线性注意力与全注意力集成困难，混合粒度与设计空间仍未充分探索。作者通过可解释性分析发现，同层不同注意力头具有功能异质性，提示头维度可能是更自然的混合粒度。

Method: 作者提出 HydraHead，沿注意力头轴混合 FA 与 LA。核心包括：基于可解释性的头选择策略，用于识别检索关键头并仅在这些头保留 FA；尺度归一化融合模块，用于缓解 FA 与 LA 头输出之间的分布差异；同时采用三阶段迁移流程，通过参数复用与蒸馏降低训练开销。

Result: 在统一训练设置下，HydraHead 在长上下文任务上优于其他混合设计，同时保持较强通用推理能力。基于可解释性选择头后，它以 7:1 的 LA:FA 比例达到 3:1 按层混合模型的长上下文性能。仅用 15B tokens 训练时，在 512K 上下文长度下相对基线提升超过 69%，接近同规模且原生 256K 上下文长度的 Qwen3.5。

Conclusion: 头级混合注意力能更精细地保留检索关键能力并降低计算成本，相比传统层级混合具有更高效率与扩展潜力；HydraHead 证明了注意力头维度是融合异构注意力机制的有效设计粒度。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of attention poses a critical bottleneck for long-context processing, spurring interest in hybrid attention designs. Most open-source hybrid models adopt a layer-wise strategy. Yet, prior work has noted the inherent difficulty of integrating Linear Attention (LA) with Full Attention (FA), suggesting that the design space of attention hybridization remains underexplored. To probe this space, we conduct interpretability analysis and observe that layers exhibit block-wise functional similarity, while individual heads within the same layer display distinct functional specialization despite sharing input features. This head-level heterogeneity suggests that the head dimension provides a natural and principled granularity for fusing heterogeneous attention signals. Building on this insight, we introduce HydraHead, a novel architecture that hybridizes FA and LA along the head axis. HydraHead features two key innovations: (1) an interpretability-driven selection strategy that identifies retrieval-critical heads and preserves FA only for them, and (2) a scale-normalized fusion module that reconciles the distributional gap between FA and LA head outputs. By leveraging a three-stage transfer pipeline with parameter reuse and distillation, we achieve high-performance hybrid models with minimal training overhead. Under a unified training setup, HydraHead outperforms other hybrid designs in long-context tasks while maintaining strong general reasoning. With interpretability-driven head selection, it matches a 3:1 layer-wise hybrid's long-context performance at a 7:1 LA-to-FA ratio. Crucially, trained on only 15B tokens, HydraHead achieves over 69% improvement over the baseline at 512K context length, approaching Qwen3.5, a leading model of comparable size with a native context length of 256K. This highlights the significant scaling potential of head-level hybridization.

</details>


### [145] [When Does Streaming Tool Use Help? Characterizing Tool-Intent Stabilization in Streaming Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20113)
*Elroy Galbraith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并测量 Streaming RAG 中“工具意图稳定化”现象，用于判断查询在用户输入尚未结束前是否已足以稳定检索到答案相关结果；在 CRAG 验证集上发现相当比例查询可提前隐藏工具延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Streaming RAG 工作主要报告总体延迟收益，但其收益本质上取决于单个查询是否能在输入完成前确定正确工具查询。作者希望刻画这种查询内在属性，解释何时投机式检索真正有效。

Method: 作者定义 tool-intent stabilization，即输入流中投机查询检索结果收敛到含答案结果的时间点；在 CRAG 1371 个验证问题上测量稳定化分布，推导与模型无关的可隐藏延迟上界 H，并用实际流式流水线验证，同时分析可预测早/晚稳定化的查询属性。

Result: 在 L=600ms、δ=3词/秒、θ=0.8 的设置下，73.9% 查询具有显著延迟隐藏潜力；在 21.3% gold evidence 原文存在且 BM25 可检索的问题子集上，95.2% 可流式化。该有利子集的充分稳定化比例 φ_suf 约在 [0.26, 0.281]，表明显著偏早；问题类型对早/晚稳定有显著但较粗粒度影响。

Conclusion: Streaming RAG 的延迟收益可由工具意图稳定化这一查询级属性解释和约束。该分析无需模型训练、可在普通 CPU 上运行，并可指导何时使用学习型投机触发器以获得更高性价比。

Abstract: Streaming Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Streaming RAG) reduces user-perceived latency by issuing tool queries in parallel with ongoing user input, before the utterance is complete. Reported gains are aggregate, yet the mechanism's benefit is fundamentally query-intrinsic: speculation can only help when the correct tool query becomes determinable before the user stops speaking or typing. We isolate and measure this property -- tool-intent stabilization, the point in the input stream at which a speculative query's retrieval converges to the answer-bearing result. On the CRAG benchmark (1371 validation questions) we (i) measure the distribution of stabilization, (ii) derive a model-agnostic bound H on the portion of tool latency that can be hidden behind the user's remaining input, as a function of tool latency L and input cadence δ, (iii) validate against a working streaming pipeline that realized savings meet or exceed this bound, and (iv) identify which query properties predict early versus late stabilization. The study requires no model training and runs on commodity CPU hardware. We find that at a realistic operating point (L=600ms, δ=3w/s, θ=0.8), 73.9% of queries across the full benchmark admit substantial latency hiding -- a blended figure that mixes sufficiency stabilization on the 21.3% of questions where gold evidence is verbatim-present and BM25-retrievable (95.2% streamable on this favorable slice) with a grounding-free top-1-settling fallback on the remainder. On the favorable slice, φ_suf is bracketed to [0.26, 0.281] by exact and relaxed grounding -- both early. Question type produces a significant but coarse early/late split (Kruskal-Wallis p=0.017, epsilon^2=0.04), directly informing when a learned speculative trigger is worth its cost.

</details>


### [146] [From Texts to Scores: Tracing the Emergence of Essay Quality Representations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20152)
*Jiaxu Zuo,Mu You,Kaixin Lan,Tao Fang,Yujia Huo,Henghua Shen,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了多种大语言模型在自动作文评分任务中的隐藏表征，发现作文质量信息主要以线性可解码形式编码，并具有一定跨提示迁移性和可解释的神经元层面特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 已显著推动自动作文评分的发展，但其内部如何表征和利用作文质量信息仍不清楚。论文旨在揭示 LLM-based AES 的内部机制与可解释性基础。

Method: 作者在两个英语作文数据集 ASAP++、CSEE 和一个葡萄牙语数据集 ENEM 上分析八个 LLM 的隐藏表征，采用线性探针、非线性探针、跨提示泛化、降维分析以及神经元级相关性与干预分析等方法。

Result: 实验表明，作文质量信息在 LLM 表征中以线性可访问形式稳定存在，并随网络层数逐步形成；该表征对不同提示策略较鲁棒，并能在不同作文题目间部分迁移。非线性探针相较线性探针仅带来有限且不稳定的提升。研究还发现若干与作文分数强相关的“作文评分神经元”，其激活对干预敏感；此外，较长作文更依赖深层网络中的相关神经元。

Conclusion: 论文认为 LLM 内部存在结构化的作文质量表征，这些表征大多可被线性解码，并在层级和神经元层面呈现可解释模式，为理解和解释 LLM 自动作文评分系统提供了新的证据与视角。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have substantially transformed Automated Essay Scoring (AES), yet the internal mechanisms underlying LLM-based scoring remain poorly understood. In this work, we systematically analyze the hidden representations of eight LLMs across two English essay datasets (ASAP++, CSEE) and one Portuguese dataset (ENEM). Using linear probing, cross-prompt generalization, dimensionality reduction, and neuron-level analyses, we find consistent evidence that essay quality information is encoded in a linearly accessible form within LLM representations. These representations emerge progressively across layers, remain robust across prompting strategies, and partially transfer across essay prompts despite differences in scoring rubrics. In addition, nonlinear probes provide only marginal and inconsistent improvements over linear probes, suggesting that most essay quality information is already linearly decodable. We further identify individual ``essay scoring neurons'' whose activations strongly correlate with essay scores and whose behavior is sensitive to targeted intervention. Moreover, the layer-wise distribution of these neurons systematically shifts with essay length, with longer essays relying more heavily on deeper layers. Overall, our findings provide evidence that LLMs encode structured representations related to essay quality and offer new insights into the interpretability of LLM-based AES systems.

</details>


### [147] [MedRLM: Recursive Multimodal Health Intelligence for Long-Context Clinical Reasoning, Sensor-Guided Screening, Evidence-Grounded Decision Support, and Community-to-Tertiary Referral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20164)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 MedRLM：一种递归式多模态健康智能框架，用于长上下文临床推理、传感器引导筛查与分级转诊支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床决策需要整合异构、纵向患者信息；现有医疗大模型与 RAG 系统多依赖单步提示或单次检索，难以处理分散在长电子病历、医学影像、传感器流、指南和转诊约束中的证据，因而鲁棒性不足、可审计性有限。

Method: MedRLM 将患者病例视为可外部交互的临床环境，通过递归检查、任务分解、检索、验证与综合进行推理；协调临床文本、纵向 EHR、医学影像、生理传感信号、指南检索、不确定性审计和转诊规划等专门代理；构建 Clinical Evidence Graph Memory 连接患者观察、检索证据、标准化定义、传感器生物标志物与转诊标准；并使用传感器引导的递归触发和不确定性门控细化机制。

Result: 摘要未报告实证结果，而是提出了评估设计：拟使用公共及需授权的临床数据集，覆盖 EHR、放射影像、ECG、ICU 时间序列与转诊代理结局，以验证框架在多模态临床推理和工作流支持中的表现。

Conclusion: MedRLM 旨在推动医疗 AI 从静态问答转向可审计、多模态、长上下文且贴近临床工作流的决策支持系统，但其有效性仍需真实数据实验和临床场景验证。

Abstract: Real-world clinical decision support requires reasoning over heterogeneous and longitudinal patient information rather than answering isolated medical questions. However, current medical large language models and retrieval-augmented generation systems often rely on single-step prompting or retrieval, which can be fragile when clinical evidence is distributed across long electronic health records, medical images, sensor streams, guidelines, and referral constraints. This paper proposes MedRLM, a Recursive Multimodal Health Intelligence framework for long-context clinical reasoning, sensor-guided screening, and community-to-tertiary referral support. Instead of compressing all patient information into one prompt, MedRLM treats the patient case as an external clinical environment that can be recursively inspected, decomposed, retrieved, verified, and synthesized. The framework coordinates specialized agents for clinical text, longitudinal EHR, medical imaging, physiological sensor signals, guideline retrieval, uncertainty auditing, and referral planning. It further introduces a Clinical Evidence Graph Memory to connect patient-specific observations with retrieved evidence, standardized definitions, sensor-derived biomarkers, and referral criteria. A sensor-guided recursive triggering mechanism activates deeper reasoning when abnormal physiological or behavioral patterns are detected, while uncertainty-gated refinement supports clinician review for high-risk or low-confidence cases. We also outline a real-data evaluation design using public and credentialed clinical datasets spanning EHR, radiology, ECG, ICU time series, and referral-proxy outcomes. MedRLM aims to move medical AI from static question answering toward auditable, multimodal, and workflow-aware clinical decision support.

</details>


### [148] [ReNikud: Audio-Supervised Hebrew Grapheme-to-Phoneme Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20179)
*Maxim Melichov,Yakov Kolani,Morris Alper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReNikud，一种面向现代希伯来语的 G2P 方法，通过弱音频监督和伪元音化架构，直接生成更贴近日常口语的 IPA 音素转写，并在多个基准上超过已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代希伯来语采用辅音音素文字体系，元音通常不书写，导致 G2P 转换高度歧义。传统先预测 nikud 元音附标再转 IPA 的方法依赖稀缺且昂贵的标注数据，缺少词重音等信息，并偏向正式语法而非真实口语；直接序列到序列 IPA 预测又难以在低资源条件下学习，并且未充分利用 abjad 文字的字符级对齐特性。

Method: ReNikud 包含两个核心设计：其一，利用基于音素的 ASR 伪标注流水线，从数千小时未标注希伯来语音频中获得弱监督音素转写，使训练信号更接近自然口语发音；其二，采用伪元音化架构，在每个字符位置预测 IPA 音素，从而显式引入字符级对齐的归纳偏置。

Result: 在已有希伯来语 G2P benchmark 以及新构建的面向口语希伯来语的 MILIM benchmark 上，ReNikud 均超过此前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将大规模未标注语音的弱监督信息与符合 abjad 书写特性的字符级对齐建模结合，可以有效提升低资源、高歧义语言的 G2P 性能，并对希伯来语 TTS 与相关语音技术具有实用价值。

Abstract: Grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) conversion for Modern Hebrew is needed for applications like text-to-speech (TTS), but is challenging due to the language's abjad writing system, which leaves vowels largely unwritten, creating substantial ambiguity. Standard approaches first predict vowel diacritics (nikud) to produce International Phonetic Alphabet (IPA) transcriptions, but this is limited: vocalization data is scarce and laborious to produce, it does not specify features such as lexical stress, and it reflects formal grammatical rules rather than everyday spoken pronunciation. Direct sequence-to-sequence IPA prediction, meanwhile, struggles on limited data and fails to exploit the character-level alignment characteristic of abjads. Our method, ReNikud, overcomes these limitations with two key insights: (1) Weak audio supervision via a phoneme-based automatic speech recognition (ASR) pseudo-labeling pipeline on thousands of hours of unlabeled Hebrew audio, yielding phonemic transcriptions that reflect natural spoken norms without manual annotation. (2) A pseudo-vocalization architecture that predicts IPA phonemes at each character position, enforcing character-level alignment as an inductive bias. Results on existing Hebrew G2P benchmarks and the new targeted MILIM benchmark for spoken Hebrew show that ReNikud surpasses previous state-of-the-art methods. We will release our code and trained models to support further work on Hebrew TTS and speech technologies.

</details>


### [149] [Pitch Spelling Jazz Lead Sheets, Solo Transcriptions, Classical Piano and Monophonic Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20198)
*Augustin Bouquillard,Florent Jacquemard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向MIDI类输入的联合音高拼写与调号估计算法，通过两阶段优化生成更合适的乐谱记谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动转录和数字乐谱处理中，MIDI音高缺少音名、升降号、调号与局部音阶等记谱信息，直接影响乐谱可读性、音乐分析、教学与文化遗产保存；尤其在爵士独奏转录等场景中，需要可靠地从音高序列推断规范记谱。

Method: 算法输入包含半音音高与小节边界。第一阶段为“modal”阶段：为每个小节估计可能的局部音阶，并通过最短路径搜索最小化乐谱中需打印的临时升降号数量。第二阶段为“tonal”阶段：利用局部音阶联合估计全局调号与各音符音名，以获得整首作品层面的最佳记谱结果。作者还定义了常见爵士音阶之间的新距离度量。

Result: 作者在多类数字乐谱数据集上评估方法，包括爵士Real Book主旋律谱、爵士独奏和低音线录音转录、传统曲调，以及钢琴和单声部乐器的古典乐谱。摘要表明该方法在多样化音乐材料上进行了验证，并适用于音乐转录与记谱处理任务。

Conclusion: 该算法通过联合建模局部音阶、全局调号与音名选择，能够改进自动音乐转录中的乐谱生成质量，并可能扩展到其他音乐记谱处理任务；提出的爵士音阶距离也具有潜在音乐学研究价值。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for pitch spelling and key estimation. Given an input in MIDI-like format, containing information on note pitches (expressed in semitones relative to the lowest reference note) and bar boundaries, it estimates the appropriate note names, a global Key Signature, and a local scale for each bar. This related information elements are evaluated jointly during two stages of optimisation. During an initial 'modal' stage, a probable scale is proposed for each bar, minimising the number of accidentals to be printed in the printed score with a shortest-path search. Then, during a second stage called 'tonal', these local scales are used to estimate the Key Signature and note names that would result in the best musical notation for the entire piece. We present evaluations conducted on datasets comprising a variety of digital musical scores: jazz lead sheets taken from the Real Book, transcriptions of recordings of jazz soli and bass lines, traditional tunes, as well as classical scores for piano and monophonic instruments. Our procedure was originally designed for use in music transcription, specifically for building digital collections of jazz solos transcribed from audio recordings, for the purposes of music analysis, teaching and the preservation of cultural heritage. This method should also prove useful for other tasks related to the processing of musical notation. Furthermore, to this end, we have defined new distances between various common jazz scales, which may be of some interest to musicological studies.

</details>


### [150] [CzechDocs: A Multiway Parallel Dataset of Formatted Documents for Minority Languages in Czechia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20212)
*Josef Jon,Ondřej Bojar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CzechDocs：一个包含 HTML、DOCX、PDF 格式的多语平行文档数据集，用于评估机器翻译在翻译时保持文档格式的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器翻译评测多关注文本质量，而对真实文档翻译中格式保留能力的系统性评估不足；因此需要一个覆盖多文件格式、多语言且可用于格式保持翻译评测的数据集与工具。

Method: 作者构建了覆盖捷克语及在捷克使用的多种语言的多向平行格式化文档数据集，主要包括乌克兰语和英语，并包含少量其他语言；数据格式涵盖 HTML、DOCX 和 PDF。同时在验证集上比较常见的格式保持机器翻译方法，并公开验证集与评测工具包。

Result: 论文产出了 CzechDocs 数据集、公开验证划分和评测工具，并给出了若干主流格式保持机器翻译方法在验证子集上的对比结果；保留测试集将用于未来共享任务。

Conclusion: CzechDocs 为文档级、格式保持机器翻译研究提供了标准化资源与评测基础，有助于推动机器翻译系统从纯文本翻译扩展到真实格式化文档场景。

Abstract: We present CzechDocs, a multiway parallel dataset of formatted documents (HTML, DOCX, and PDF) covering Czech and minority languages used in Czechia-primarily Ukrainian and English, with smaller portions of Vietnamese, Russian and other languages. The dataset is designed to support the evaluation of machine translation systems that aim to preserve document formatting during translation. We provide a comparison of the most common approaches to format-preserving machine translation on a validation subset of the dataset. This validation split, together with the evaluation toolkit, is publicly released for further research. A held-out test split will be reserved for a future shared task focused on document-level translation with formatting preservation.

</details>


### [151] [Actionable Activation Directions for Detecting and Mitigating Emergent Misalignment Across Language Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20225)
*Abdul Rafay Syed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究不安全代码微调导致的语言模型“涌现性失准”是否对应可因果干预的激活空间方向。结果显示，模型内部存在高度可分且可操控的失准方向，但跨架构迁移虽能抑制行为却缺乏特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不安全代码微调会诱发模型在更广泛任务中的失准行为，但其内部机制尚不清楚。作者希望判断这种失准是否可被表示为不同模型架构之间共享的线性激活方向，并进一步评估该方向是否具备因果可操作性与跨模型可迁移性。

Method: 作者在四个指令微调模型家族（Qwen2.5-1.5B、Gemma-2-2B、Llama-3.2-1B、Ministral-3-3B）上进行相同微调设置，比较对齐与失准模型在最终层激活上的差异。通过均值差方向进行线性分离，并采用激活 steering（减去该方向）测试因果效果；同时使用安全代码控制条件检验内容特异性。跨架构部分使用 ridge regression 将方向映射到其他模型空间，并与随机、正交方向对照比较。

Result: 最终层均值差方向能以 99.6% 的准确率区分对齐与失准激活。模型内因果 steering 可将代码相关 spillover 降低 21–51 个百分点，且安全代码控制表明该方向具有内容特异性。跨架构映射也能显著抑制失准行为，最高降低 46 个百分点，但随机和正交方向表现相近，说明其缺乏方向特异性。作者还观察到非对称迁移拓扑：Gemma 和 Qwen 更像几何“供体”，Llama 更像“接收体”。

Conclusion: 论文认为失准在模型内部可由线性激活方向捕获，并且该方向具有因果可操作性；但跨架构线性校正存在明显限制，虽然可能产生行为抑制，却不能证明迁移方向具有特异因果含义。因此，模型安全审计更应依赖模型内部 probing 与干预，而非简单假设跨架构共享线性修正方向。

Abstract: Fine-tuning language models on insecure code induces emergent misalignment with poorly understood internal structure. We investigate whether this misalignment corresponds to a causally actionable activation-space direction shared across architectures. Across four instruction-tuned model families (Qwen2.5-1.5B, Gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-1B, Ministral-3-3B) finetuned identically, a difference-in-means direction achieves 99.6% separation of aligned and misaligned activations at each model's final layer. Causal steering by subtracting this direction reduces code spillover by 21-51 points, while a secure-code control confirms content specificity. Cross-architecture transfer via ridge regression maps yields large behavioral suppression (up to 46 points) but fails specificity controls as random and orthogonal directions perform comparably. We identify a two-tier specificity structure: within-model directions are causally specific and actionable; cross-model directions are causally real but non-specific. An asymmetric transfer topology emerges, with Gemma and Qwen acting as geometric donors and Llama as a receiver. These findings define the limits of linear cross-architecture correction and recommend within-model probing for auditing.

</details>


### [152] [The Register Gap: A Meaning Intelligence Framework for Nigerian Public Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20255)
*Celestine Achi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Meaning Intelligence Framework（MIF），用于尼日利亚公共话语的九维标注与评估，强调区分表层情感与真实交际意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有尼日利亚语言情感基准多将任务简化为正/负/中三分类，难以处理同一句话在不同说话者、受众与情境下呈现相反语用力量的问题；作者认为主要瓶颈是语境理解失败而非翻译失败。

Method: 构建九个评分维度：语域、表层情感、真实意图、讽刺、编码潜台词、风险等级、标注者置信度、说话者情绪和建议沟通行动；制作覆盖标准英语、尼日利亚英语、尼日利亚皮钦语及代码混合语域的30条校准数据集，并比较 Gemini 2.5 Flash 在零样本与提供MIF schema上下文提示下的表现。

Result: 发现“语域差距”：零样本语域分类准确率为33.3%，提供MIF schema后升至73.3%，提升40个百分点。综合 Meaning Intelligence Score 从73.2升至78.6，提升5.4分；主要收益来自语域识别、编码潜台词检测（+10分）和战略行动建议（+10.3分）。

Conclusion: MIF可作为面向尼日利亚公共话语的更细粒度语用理解框架，相比传统情感三分类更能捕捉真实交际意图；但当前实证规模较小，需依赖更大公开与私有保留集进一步验证泛化性。

Abstract: We introduce the Meaning Intelligence Framework (MIF), a nine-dimension annotation and evaluation schema for Nigerian public discourse that separates surface sentiment from true communicative intent. Existing benchmarks for Nigerian languages, including NaijaSenti and AfriSenti, treat sentiment classification as a three-way polarity task (positive, negative, neutral). We argue that the dominant failure mode of AI systems on Nigerian discourse is not translation failure but context failure: the same utterance carries opposite pragmatic force depending on speaker, audience, and situation. The MIF operationalises this insight across nine scored dimensions: register, surface sentiment, true intent, irony, coded subtext, risk tier, annotator confidence, speaker emotion, and recommended communications action. We construct a 30-item calibration dataset spanning Standard English, Nigerian English, Nigerian Pidgin, and code-mixed registers, and evaluate a frontier language model (Gemini 2.5 Flash) under zero-shot and schema-informed prompting conditions. The headline finding is the Register Gap: zero-shot register classification accuracy is 33.3%, rising to 73.3% (+40 points) when the model receives the MIF schema in-context. The composite Meaning Intelligence Score increases by 5.4 points (73.2 to 78.6) under schema-informed prompting, with the largest practical gains in register identification, coded-subtext detection (+10 points), and strategic action recommendation (+10.3 points). We release the framework specification, annotation guidelines, and the 30-item public calibration set to support reproducibility, while retaining a private holdout corpus for contamination-protected evaluation.

</details>


### [153] [PsyScore: A Psychometrically-Aware Framework for Trait-Adaptive Essay Scoring and ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20287)
*Wei Xia,Jin Wu,Haoran Shi,Xiangyu Wang,Chanjin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PsyScore 将自动作文评分与个性化教学反馈统一到共享的潜在能力表征中，在保证评分竞争力的同时提升反馈的教学适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AES 方法通常将评分与反馈割裂处理：神经评分模型可解释性有限，LLM 反馈又常忽视学习者能力水平，导致评估结果难以直接转化为有效、分层的教学支持。

Method: 提出 PsyScore 框架，包含三部分：1）Trait-Adaptive Neural IRT Scorer，将 GPCM 融入神经架构以估计学生能力并保留心理测量解释性；2）ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback Generator，依据诊断出的能力参数调节多智能体反馈策略，实现面向不同水平学生的支架式反馈；3）Multi-Perspective Feedback Evaluation Strategy，通过成对偏好判断与学生修改模拟评估反馈质量。

Result: 在 ASAP++ 数据集上的实验表明，PsyScore 在评分性能上具有竞争力，并能生成更符合教学需求和学习者水平的反馈。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将心理测量诊断、神经评分和能力自适应反馈生成结合起来，可缓解 AES 中评分与反馈分离的问题，并增强系统的可解释性与教学实用性。

Abstract: Effective Automated Essay Scoring (AES) are expected to support both reliable assessment and actionable instructional feedback. However, existing approaches often treat scoring and feedback as separate components: neural scoring models provide limited interpretability, while Large Language Model (LLM)-based feedback is typically insensitive to learners proficiency levels. To address this fragmentation, this work proposes PsyScore, a psychometrically-aware framework that integrates diagnostic assessment with instructional scaffolding through a shared latent ability representation. PsyScore comprises three key modules: a Trait-Adaptive Neural IRT Scorer that incorporates the Graded Partial Credit Model (GPCM) into a neural architecture, enabling the precise estimation of student ability while maintaining psychometric interpretability, a ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback Generator, which conditions multi-agent feedback strategies on the diagnosed ability parameter to adapt instructional focus across different proficiency levels, and a Multi-Perspective Feedback Evaluation Strategy that assesses feedback quality via pairwise preference judgements and student revision simulations. Experiments on the ASAP++ dataset demonstrate that PsyScore achieves competitive scoring performance while providing more pedagogically aligned feedback.

</details>


### [154] [CATCH-ME if you RAG: a dataset of Contextually Annotated multi-Turn Counterspeech against Hate and Misinformation Exchanges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20369)
*Helena Bonaldi,Genoveffa Martone,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个大规模、专家策划的多语言对话数据集，用于应对仇恨言论与虚假信息交叉场景，并支持事实依据与RAG应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP研究多将仇恨言论与虚假信息分开处理；LLM零样本生成的反驳话语常重复、空泛；相关高质量、多轮、多语言数据资源稀缺。

Method: 构建首个大规模、专家策划、多语言对话数据集，覆盖五种语言和七类边缘化群体；对话基于已验证外部知识，如事实核查文章和NGO报告，并提供文档级与片段级跨度标注。

Result: 该资源可用于训练与评估更具说服力、事实扎根的反驳话语生成模型，并可直接服务于检索增强生成系统。

Conclusion: 论文通过补齐仇恨言论与虚假信息交叉场景下多语言、多轮、事实支撑数据的空白，为更可靠的自动化反驳话语研究提供基础资源。

Abstract: Online hate speech and misinformation frequently overlap, yet NLP research has mainly treated them in isolation. While LLMs represent a scalable solution for assisting humans in the generation of counterspeech for both threats, zero-shot models frequently generate repetitive and vague responses, underscoring the need for high-quality examples to steer model generation. However, existing counterspeech datasets against the overlap of hate and misinformation are scarce and limited to single-turn English dialogues, while real-life interactions span across multiple turns and languages. To bridge this gap, we introduce the first large-scale, expert-curated, multilingual dataset of dialogues tackling the intersection of hate and misinformation. To ensure factual grounding, the dialogues are also anchored in verified external knowledge (i.e., fact-checking articles and NGO reports) and include document- and chunk-level span annotations, making it directly applicable for RAG systems. Covering five languages and targeting hate directed at seven marginalized groups, this novel resource enables the training and evaluation of more persuasive, factually grounded counterspeech models.

</details>


### [155] [Your Mouse and Eyes Secretly Leak Your Preference: LLM Alignment using Implicit Feedback from Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20482)
*Haw-Shiuan Chang,Jeffrey Gomez,Mehul Patwari,Aryan Sajith,Hamed Zamani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证利用用户隐式反馈（鼠标轨迹与眼动注视）来改进LLM对齐的价值，构建IFLLM数据集，并显示其可显著提升奖励模型与DPO后的响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法主要依赖显式人工偏好反馈训练奖励模型，但显式反馈稀缺且标注成本高；同时，这些方法未充分利用现实交互中大量存在、且在互联网产品中被证明有价值的隐式用户反馈。

Method: 作者构建IFLLM数据集：收集59名Mechanical Turk工作者针对LLM回答的1336个多轮问题交互数据，包括用户问题、对LLM响应的鼠标轨迹以及通过摄像头获取的眼动注视点；随后基于这些隐式反馈训练奖励模型，并与仅基于文本的奖励模型结合/比较，再将其用于DPO对八个LLM进行优化评估。

Result: IFLLM显示用户注视行为和鼠标轨迹具有高度多样性。加入隐式反馈后，奖励模型准确率从55%提升到64%；在八个LLM上应用DPO后，相对响应质量提升接近增加两倍。

Conclusion: 隐式用户反馈可为LLM对齐提供有效信号，能弥补显式偏好反馈稀缺的问题，并显著增强奖励建模与DPO优化效果，说明其在真实应用场景中具有重要价值。

Abstract: To align a Large Language Model (LLM), most existing methods collect explicit human feedback and train a reward model to predict the human preference based on the response text. These existing methods have two key limitations. First, the users rarely provide explicit feedback for LLM responses, which makes the high-quality preference annotation expensive to collect. Second, the methods do not leverage implicit human feedback, which has proven vital to the economic moats of Internet giants. To quantify the value of implicit feedback, we build a new dataset called IFLLM, which collects 1336 multi-turn questions from the 59 Mechanical Turk workers, their mouse trajectories, and eye gazing points to the LLMs' responses from their webcams. IFLLM shows that the users have very diverse types of gazing behavior and mouse trajectories. Our reward model based on the implicit user feedback boosts the accuracy of the text-based reward model from 55% to 64% and nearly triples the relative response quality improvements after applying the DPO to eight LLMs, demonstrating the value of implicit feedback in the wild. Our data collection website, dataset, and codes can be found at https://github.com/themehulpatwari/llm-implicit-feedback/.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Global Replanning: Hierarchical Recovery for Cross-Device Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20487)
*Shu Yao,Yuhua Luo,Qian Long,Jingru Fan,Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuheng Wang,Lin Wu,Yufan Dang,Huatao Li,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 H-RePlan，一个面向多设备智能体的层次化重规划框架，通过区分设备内策略恢复与全局跨设备重规划，提高动态故障下任务完成的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实计算机使用任务常跨越多个应用和设备，运行时故障频繁且异质。现有多设备智能体通常采用重复尝试、重新分配子任务或修改全局计划等粗粒度恢复方式，缺乏对设备本地可替代执行策略空间的系统建模，难以判断故障应在本设备内修复还是需要全局重规划。

Method: 作者提出 H-RePlan：为每个设备配置可互换的执行策略，并通过紧凑的跨层故障抽象，将设备本地策略级恢复与编排器层面的全局重规划分离；同时支持统一的 API、CLI、GUI 执行。为评估该能力，构建 HeraBench，一个在 Linux 与 Android 设备上生成跨设备工作流并注入策略级和设备级故障的基准。

Result: 实验显示，H-RePlan 相比单策略方法和粗粒度多设备基线，在任务完成率、指令遵循度和 perfect-pass rate 上显著更优，同时降低了实现可靠端到端成功所需的 token 成本。

Conclusion: 范围感知的层次化恢复机制对于鲁棒的多设备智能体执行至关重要；通过先进行设备内策略恢复、必要时再触发全局重规划，可以更有效处理复杂跨设备任务中的动态故障。

Abstract: Real-world computer-use tasks often span multiple applications and devices, requiring agents to coordinate heterogeneous environments under dynamic runtime failures. Existing multi-device agent systems support task decomposition and cross-device assignment, but recovery remains largely coarse-grained: when execution fails, they typically retry the same strategy, reassign the subtask, or revise the global plan, without systematically modeling the device-local strategy space. This limits their ability to distinguish failures that can be repaired within the current device from those that require cross-device replanning. We propose \textbf{H-RePlan}, a hierarchical replanning framework for multi-device agents with unified API--CLI--GUI execution. H-RePlan equips each device with interchangeable execution strategies and separates device-local strategy recovery from orchestrator-level global replanning through a compact cross-layer failure abstraction. To evaluate this capability, we introduce \textbf{HeraBench}, a fault-injected benchmark that constructs cross-device workflows over Linux and Android devices and injects strategy- and device-level failures. Experiments show that H-RePlan substantially outperforms single-strategy and coarse-grained multi-device baselines, achieving higher completion, instruction adherence, and perfect-pass rates while reducing the token cost required for reliable end-to-end success. These results demonstrate that scope-aware hierarchical recovery is essential for robust multi-device agent execution.

</details>


### [157] [StylisticBias: A Few Human Visual Cues Drive Most Social Biases in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20527)
*Shaghayegh Kolli,Timo Cavelius,Nafiseh Nikeghbal,Samantha Dalal,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 StylisticBias 基准，通过固定人脸身份并逐一改变视觉属性，评估多模态大语言模型在社会判断中的属性级偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常比较不同个体或群体，难以区分模型判断差异究竟来自身份差异还是外观属性差异；而 MLLM 在重要场景中被使用，其受视觉线索影响的机制仍不清楚。

Method: 构建 500 张写实基础人脸，并为每张人脸生成约 50 种单属性变体，总计约 2.5 万张图像；在保持身份不变的前提下，每次仅改变一个视觉属性。使用 6 个 MLLM，在 25 个二元社会判断场景中评估不同视觉线索对模型输出的影响。

Result: 年龄和体型主导身份层面的影响；时尚风格及其他视觉线索造成最大的属性层面判断变化。约 15 个属性解释了近 80% 的总变异，说明偏差集中在少数视觉线索上。模型在与外观语义高度相关的判断中最敏感，尤其是社会经济和风格相关判断。

Conclusion: StylisticBias 为多模态模型的细粒度偏差评估提供了可控基准，揭示了少量关键视觉属性对模型社会判断的显著影响，并支持后续偏差诊断与缓解研究。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in personally and societally consequential settings, yet the visual cues that shape how these models judge people remain poorly understood. Prior work often compares different (groups of) individuals, making it difficult to separate appearance effects from identity differences. We introduce StylisticBias, a controlled benchmark for evaluating attribute-level social bias in MLLMs. We generate 500 photorealistic base faces and create about 50 single-attribute variations per face, producing about 25K images. This design keeps identity fixed and changes one visual attribute at a time. It lets us measure how specific cues shift model judgments. We evaluate six MLLMs across 25 binary social judgment scenarios. We find that age and body type dominate identity-level effects, while fashion style and other visual cues drive the largest attribute-level shifts. We further find that about 15 attributes account for nearly 80\% of the total variation, showing that bias is concentrated in a small set of visual cues. Sensitivity is strongest in judgments that are semantically aligned with appearance, especially socioeconomic and style-related judgments. We release StylisticBias as a benchmark for fine-grained bias evaluation in multimodal models. Code and dataset: https://github.com/timo-cavelius/StylisticBias and https://hf.co/datasets/shaghayegh/stylistic-bias-dataset.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Ricci flow for the Bures--Helstrom qubit metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19493)
*Andrew Lesniewski*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文显式刻画了量子比特态空间上 Bures--Helstrom 度量的 Ricci 流，证明其作为 Einstein 度量在未归一化 Ricci 流下仅发生同伦收缩，并在体积归一化流下成为固定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bures--Helstrom 度量是量子比特态空间上最小单调 Riemann 度量，并可与三维单位圆球面的测地半球对应。研究其 Ricci 流有助于理解该量子信息几何度量在几何演化下的稳定性、奇性形成及其是否保持在单调度量锥内。

Method: 作者采用旋转对称度量表示，分析 Ricci 流中径向 lapse 与 warping factor 的耦合系统；进一步通过 Hamilton--DeTurck 规范化将问题化为单个标量方程。在移动 DeTurck 框架下，令平方 warping 函数 Ψ=Φ²，得到线性带强迫项的热方程 D_tΨ=Ψ_ss−2，并比较固定 lapse 坐标中的输运项形式。

Result: 由于 Bures--Helstrom 度量是 Einstein 度量，未归一化 Ricci 流给出显式同伦收缩解 g(t)=(1−4t)g_BH，标量曲率为 6/(1−4t)，消亡时间为 T=1/4。度量在所有 t<T 时保持在单调锥内，仅在塌缩极限离开非退化 Riemann 度量锥。体积归一化流下该度量为固定点，其线性化算子为 Δ_{S^3}+3，谱为 σ_l=−(l−1)(l+3)，去除尺度模后谱隙为 5。

Conclusion: 论文表明 Bures--Helstrom 度量的 Ricci 流行为高度刚性且可显式求解：未归一化流产生有限时间同伦塌缩，归一化流则保持固定，并具有明确的线性稳定谱结构。这说明该量子信息几何度量在 Ricci 流下具有良好的稳定性与几何可控性。

Abstract: The Bures--Helstrom metric is the minimal monotone Riemannian metric on the state space of a qubit. With the quantum Fisher normalization used here, it identifies the Bloch ball with a geodesic hemisphere of the unit round three--sphere. We describe its Ricci flow explicitly. In a general rotationally symmetric gauge the flow is a coupled system for the radial lapse and warping factor; a single scalar equation appears only after a Hamilton--DeTurck gauge choice. In the corresponding moving DeTurck frame the squared warping function $Ψ=Φ^2$ satisfies the linear forced heat equation \begin{equation*} D_tΨ=Ψ_{ss}-2, \end{equation*} while the fixed-lapse coordinate form contains the associated transport term. Since the Bures--Helstrom metric is Einstein, the geometric flow itself is the homothetic shrinker \begin{equation*} g(t)=(1-4t)g_{\mathrm{BH}}, \end{equation*} with scalar curvature $6/(1-4t)$ and extinction time $T=1/4$. Thus the metric remains inside the monotone cone for all $t<T$ and leaves the cone of nondegenerate Riemannian metrics only through the collapsed limit. We also record the volume--normalized flow, for which the Bures--Helstrom metric is a fixed point. Its linearization is the shifted round--sphere Laplacian $Δ_{\mathbb S^3}+3$, with spectrum \begin{equation*} σ_\ell=-(\ell-1)(\ell+3), \end{equation*} and spectral gap $5$ after removal of the scaling mode.

</details>


### [159] [Lightweight Non-Line-of-Sight Channel Detection for ML-assisted Bluetooth Direction Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19497)
*Hamed Talebian,Aamir Mahmood,Mehdi Haghshenas,Stefani Rydbloom,Peter Karlsson,Mikael Gidlund*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向 BLE 定向测距/测向场景的轻量级 LOS/NLOS 分类流程，基于 CTE IQ 特征、鲁棒标准化、核近似与 SVC，在复杂多径环境中提升通道状态识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLE 方向发现适用于室内工业定位，但窄带 BLE 信号在多径环境下角度估计易受反射和散射偏置影响。现有 LOS/NLOS 检测多面向宽带无线电，缺少适合 BLE 窄带 CTE IQ 特征的表示方法、可扩展核特征变换，以及专用标注数据集。

Method: 研究构建受控 BLE 测量平台，在两类传播环境中采集并标注 LOS/NLOS 数据；对 CTE IQ 特征进行分位数鲁棒标准化；使用 PCA 和 AKDE 分析场景统计特性与类别可分性；采用 Nyström Kernel Approximation 构造低秩非线性特征映射，并接轻量 SVC 分类头，同时与 RF 和 MLP 进行对比；最后利用校准后验概率进行代价敏感阈值选择。

Result: NKA 相比原始基线带来约 7%–14% 的准确率提升。MLP 的绝对准确率更高，但 Nyström–SVC 在训练复杂度、推理成本和内存占用之间取得更优折中，更适合资源受限的实时定位系统。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了基于 BLE CTE IQ 的数据驱动 LOS/NLOS 分类在窄带 BLE 定向定位中具有可行性，并表明核近似结合轻量分类器可在精度与部署成本之间取得较好平衡，有助于提升多径环境下 BLE 室内定位的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) direction-finding is promising for indoor industrial localization, but its accuracy degrades in multipath environments where reflections and scattering bias angle estimates. Although line-of-sight (LOS) and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) detection is well studied for wide-band radios, BLE direction-finding still lacks narrow-band channel-feature representations, scalable kernel-based feature transformations, and dedicated datasets for data-driven, lightweight channel classification. To address this gap, the work introduces a controlled BLE measurement setup that generates labeled LOS/NLOS data in two distinct propagation environments. A quality-driven machine learning (ML)-based pipeline is then developed for BLE Constant Tone Extension (CTE) In-phase-Quadrature (IQ) features. First, robust quantile-based standardization is applied to reduce the influence of outliers and heavy-tailed effects. The standardized features are then analyzed using Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Adaptive Kernel Density Estimation (AKDE) to verify scenario-dependent statistics and reveal LOS/NLOS separability. Next, Nyström Kernel Approximation (NKA) constructs low-rank nonlinear feature maps followed by a lightweight Support Vector Classifier (SVC) head for LOS/NLOS detection. This classifier is compared with Random Forest (RF) and Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) models. Results show that NKA improves accuracy by about 7-14% relative to the raw baseline. Although the MLP achieves higher absolute accuracy, the Nyström--SVC approach offers a more favorable trade-off between training complexity, inference cost, and memory footprint. Finally, several pipeline-calibrated posterior probabilities are utilized for cost-aware threshold selection and efficient real-time LOS/NLOS detection in resource-constrained localization systems.

</details>


### [160] [Parity Selection Rule for Information and Dissipation in Driven Steady States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19702)
*Mengqi Li,Lixin Li,Wensheng Lin,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文提出并证明：在旋转驱动的线性非平衡稳态中，对称信息量与熵产生之间不存在普适的紧等式，因为二者受奇偶选择规则约束；互信息在特定对齐条件下对驱动反演为偶，而熵产生对驱动为二次依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究希望在驱动稳态中建立快照互信息等对称信息量与熵产生之间的紧密等式或双向约束，但此类关系为何难以成立尚不清楚。论文旨在从对称性和奇偶性角度解释这一障碍，并给出可检验的物理预测。

Method: 研究旋转驱动的线性非平衡稳态，分析弛豫矩阵与扩散矩阵在对易或非对易条件下的快照互信息、驱动反演奇偶性和熵产生率；利用矩阵对易性、各向同性、交换子范数、迹与行列式等量推导闭式表达，并将结论扩展到各向同性稳定噪声情形。

Result: 当弛豫矩阵和扩散矩阵对易时，两个时间切片之间的快照互信息在驱动反演下严格为偶函数；若对齐被破坏，奇偶破缺随交换子范数线性增长。完全各向同性时，互信息进一步与驱动无关，二维情形闭式值约为 0.145 nats。相同对齐条件下，熵产生关于驱动严格呈二次形式，其系数可由两个矩阵的迹和行列式显式表示。

Conclusion: 由于互信息的偶扇区与熵产生相关的奇/耗散结构之间存在正交性，对称信息量与熵产生之间只能形成单侧热力学不确定性界，而不能形成紧等式。该选择规则源于漂移项的旋转对称性，并在重尾各向同性稳定噪声下仍成立，具有实验可证伪性。

Abstract: Tight equalities between symmetric information and entropy production in driven steady states remain elusive. We show that they are forbidden by a parity selection rule for rotation-driven linear nonequilibrium steady states. Whenever the relaxation and diffusion matrices commute, the snapshot mutual information between two time slices is exactly even under drive reversal, and parity violation rises linearly in the commutator norm when alignment is broken. Full isotropy strengthens this to drive-independence, and the planar mutual information takes the closed-form value of about 0.145 nats. Under the same alignment, the entropy production is exactly quadratic in the drive, and its prefactor admits an explicit closed form in the traces and determinant of the two matrices. The orthogonality of even and odd sectors leaves only one-sided thermodynamic-uncertainty bounds. The rule rests on the rotational symmetry of the drift alone and survives heavy-tailed isotropic stable noise with tail index below two, where variance-based bounds become vacuous. A falsifiable test is proposed on an electrical Brownian gyrator augmented for independent drive control with circuit-level stable-noise injection.

</details>


### [161] [Doeblin Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19859)
*Dongmin Lee,William Lu,Anuran Makur,Japneet Singh*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 Doeblin 曲线，用于比传统 Doeblin 系数更精细地刻画马尔可夫核的多路信息收缩，即使 Doeblin 系数为 0 也可给出非平凡收缩保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Doeblin 系数可作为 Dobrushin 总变差距离收缩系数的多路推广，但通常需要系数远离 0 等强条件才能证明信息收缩；当系数为 0 时，现有工具往往只能给出空泛结论。作者希望借助非线性信息收缩思想，建立更细粒度的 Doeblin 型刻画。

Method: 作者引入 Doeblin 曲线这一非线性函数，用于在给定散度水平与幂约束下量化马尔可夫核对输入分布族的收缩行为；同时给出 Doeblin 系数的新变分表征，研究 Doeblin 曲线的性质，定义多种幂约束 Doeblin 曲线，并基于变分表征推导上下界。

Result: 所提出的 Doeblin 曲线能够在 Doeblin 系数为 0 的信道上仍提供非平凡的收缩保证，并在噪声迭代优化的泛化界、噪声电路可靠计算误差界、在线迭代算法的差分隐私保证等问题中扩展已有结果。

Conclusion: Doeblin 曲线比单一 Doeblin 系数更能揭示多路信息收缩的细粒度结构，可作为分析马尔可夫核、噪声算法与隐私/可靠性问题的有效工具。

Abstract: Recent research on Doeblin coefficients has shed light on their usefulness as a multi-way generalization of the Dobrushin contraction coefficient for TV distance, in a separate vein from their classic role in the theory of Markov chain ergodicity. However, strong conditions, such as being bounded away from 0, are typically necessary for Doeblin coefficients to establish the existence of information contraction. Building on recently formulated concepts of nonlinear information contraction, we aim to propose a finer-grained Doeblin-based characterization of multi-way contraction behavior which yields non-vacuous contraction guarantees even for channels whose Doeblin coefficient is 0. To this end, we introduce the notion of a Doeblin curve -- a nonlinear function which quantifies the contraction behavior of a Markov kernel on collections of input distributions at specific levels of divergence and power. Through the course of our analysis, we develop a new variational characterization of Doeblin coefficients, present several properties of Doeblin curves, define several versions of power-constrained Doeblin curves, and derive upper and lower bounds using our aforementioned variational characterization. We then utilize these results in diverse areas, including generalization bounds for noisy iterative optimization, error bounds for reliable computation with noisy circuits, and differential privacy guarantees for online iterative algorithms. In particular, we extend results in these areas to broader domains or group settings, leveraging Doeblin curves to reveal finer-grained contraction phenomena than Doeblin coefficients.

</details>


### [162] [Site-Specific MIMO Channel Generation via Diffusion and Flow Matching: Fidelity, Efficiency, and Downstream Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20098)
*Sina Beyraghi,Masoud Sadeghian,Firdous Bin Ismail,Angel Lozano,Paul Almasan,Giovanni Geraci*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究利用位置条件生成模型合成高质量、站点特定的 MIMO 信道数据，以降低真实测量数据采集成本；比较 cDDIM 与 cFMM，发现二者均能较好保持站点传播特性，其中 cFMM 推理速度显著更快，并能提升下游物理层任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 原生无线网络需要大量真实、站点特定的 MIMO 信道数据，但大规模测量活动成本高、周期长且难以覆盖多样传播场景。因此需要一种能在少量真实数据条件下生成可靠信道样本的方法，用于数据增强和下游任务训练。

Method: 提出并比较两类以用户坐标为条件的生成范式：条件去噪扩散隐式模型 cDDIM 与条件流匹配模型 cFMM。二者均根据用户位置生成 MIMO 信道矩阵，以保留部署环境中的空间结构。评估维度包括统计保真度、生成效率以及在 CSI 压缩和波束对齐等下游任务中的实用性。

Result: 在 28 GHz 与 3.5 GHz、视距与非视距等多种传播场景下，两种模型都能准确捕捉站点特定信道特征，即使训练数据稀缺也表现良好。cFMM 在生成质量上接近 cDDIM，但推理时间约减少一个数量级。使用合成信道增强稀缺数据集后，下游物理层任务性能明显优于仅使用少量真实数据或随机信道模型。

Conclusion: 位置条件生成模型是缓解站点特定无线信道数据稀缺问题的有效手段。相比 cDDIM，cFMM 在保持生成质量的同时具备更高推理效率，更适合需要快速生成大规模信道数据的 AI 原生无线网络应用。

Abstract: This paper explores the use of generative models to synthesize high-quality, site-specific multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channel data, addressing the high cost of the extensive measurement campaigns required to acquire real-world data for AI-native wireless networks. Two location-conditioned generative paradigms are compared: a conditional denoising diffusion implicit model (cDDIM), and a conditional flow matching model (cFMM). Both these models generate MIMO channel matrices conditioned on user coordinates, to preserve the spatial structure of the deployment site. The approaches are evaluated across three dimensions: statistical fidelity (including beam consistency and effective rank), generation efficiency, and utility in downstream tasks such as channel-state information compression and beam alignment. Results across diverse propagation scenarios (28 GHz and 3.5 GHz, both line-of-sight and non-line-of-sight) demonstrate that both models accurately capture site-specific characteristics, even when trained on scarce ground-truth data. Notably, cFMM achieves a quality comparable to cDDIM with roughly an order of magnitude less inference time. Augmenting scarce site-specific datasets with these synthetic channels yields hefty performance gains in downstream physical layer tasks compared to using scarce data alone or stochastic channels.

</details>


### [163] [Spatially Robust Near-Field SWIPT Using Pinching Antennas: Rate-Energy Tradeoff Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20133)
*Zoran Hadzi-Velkov,Marija Poposka,Slavche Pejoski,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向服务区域覆盖的鲁棒PWA-SWIPT设计，通过离散天线选择提升近场信息传输与能量传输在用户位置偏移下的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近场PWA优化多针对单点坐标最大化增益，对定位误差和用户移动高度敏感；而实际SWIPT系统需要在一定服务区域内同时保障信息解码与能量采集性能。

Method: 将信息解码与能量采集接收端的服务区域离散建模，构建以最大化能量采集区域内收集能量、同时满足信息解码区域鲁棒速率约束的非凸二进制二次规划问题；采用半定松弛刻画R-E区域上界，并设计满足二进制硬件约束的低复杂度基于交换的局部搜索算法获得下界。

Result: 数值结果表明，相比点优化方法，所提覆盖导向设计能在服务区域内实现更稳定的性能，并提供鲁棒的速率-能量折中。

Conclusion: 离散天线激活与服务区域覆盖建模能够显著增强PWA近场SWIPT系统对位置偏差和用户移动的鲁棒性，适合作为实际近场无线信息与能量传输的设计框架。

Abstract: Pinching Waveguide Antennas (PWAs) offer significant potential for simultaneous wireless information and power transfer (SWIPT) by enabling precise near-field energy focusing. However, existing optimization frameworks are largely point-based (targeting a single coordinate for maximum gain), and thus highly sensitive to positioning errors and mobility, as near-field signals fluctuate significantly even over small spatial displacements. In this paper, we propose a spatially robust design framework based on discrete antenna selection optimized for service area (SA) coverage. Unlike point-based approaches, our model guarantees quality of service within predefined SAs for both information decoding (ID) and energy harvesting (EH) receivers, thereby improving robustness to user displacements. We formulate the problem as a non-convex binary quadratic program aimed at maximizing harvested energy within the EH SA subject to robust rate constraints in the ID SA. To characterize fundamental performance limits, we develop a semidefinite relaxation (SDR) framework that provides an upper bound on the achievable rate-energy (R-E) region. For the lower bound, we employ a low-complexity swap-based local search algorithm enforcing binary hardware constraints. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed coverage-oriented design yields a robust R-E tradeoff and maintains stable performance across service regions, highlighting the advantages of discrete antenna activation over point-based near-field optimization approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 AgenticRei，用于治理由大语言模型驱动的自主智能体系统，扩展传统策略引擎仅能表达允许/禁止的能力，支持义务、豁免、冲突解决和本体推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的自主智能体能够调用工具、处理数据、安装软件并跨组织协作，带来新的安全、隐私与合规治理挑战。现有策略引擎如 XACML、Rego、Cedar 主要处理访问控制中的允许/禁止，难以表达企业治理中更复杂的义务生命周期、条件性豁免、元策略优先级和领域本体层级推理。

Method: 作者提出 AgenticRei，基于 Rei 框架构建一种去义务逻辑策略语言，并用 OWL 表达策略；在运行时由独立于 LLM 的高性能逻辑引擎进行评估。该治理管线同时用于控制智能体的工具调用和智能体间消息传递，并支持基本许可/禁止、义务、豁免、策略冲突解决以及本体推理。

Result: 论文通过示例展示，AgenticRei 能表达当前生产级策略引擎大多无法表达的安全与隐私治理约束，例如动作后的通知义务、特定条件下的义务豁免、冲突策略的优先级处理，以及基于领域类别层级的推理。

Conclusion: AgenticRei 为自主智能体系统提供了比传统访问控制更完整的企业治理机制，能够在 LLM 外部以可解释、形式化的方式执行复杂政策约束，并可与 A2AS 等行业标准框架自然组合。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [165] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Saja Aldabet,Mamoun Awad,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并验证一种人机协同、可复现的课程覆盖度测量流程，用于评估计算机科学本科项目对CS2013与CS2023知识体系的覆盖情况及变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际计算机科学课程指南约每十年更新一次，但高校项目缺乏可靠、可复现的方法来量化自身对指南知识单元的覆盖程度，并区分课程实际缺口与指南结构调整带来的表观变化。

Method: 将课程项目与外部知识体系表示为结构化语料；使用语义检索生成课程到知识单元的候选匹配；由人工在明确覆盖定义下确认匹配。比较七种检索器，采用倒数排名融合集成方法表现最佳，并通过第二评分者使用Cohen's kappa验证映射一致性；进一步扩展到能力表述与认知深度评估。

Result: 该项目覆盖CS2023知识单元的49.7%、CS2013的50.9%，十年间总体覆盖率近似稳定。CS2023与CS2013映射的一致性分别为kappa 0.64与0.69。对已覆盖单元，约88%明确表述相应能力；但达到推荐认知深度的比例在CS2023下为76%，在CS2013下为95%，反映新版指南提高了期望。长期缺口包括并行与分布式计算、程序设计语言基础、系统基础等。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可复用的人机协同评估工具，能够纵向比较课程项目对不同版本课程指南的覆盖，区分项目固有结构性不足与标准演化带来的差异，并提示检索器选择需经过实证评估。

Abstract: Undergraduate computer science is governed by international curricular guidelines revised about once a decade, yet programs lack a reliable, reproducible way to measure how completely they cover the current guidelines and how that coverage shifts when the guidelines are restructured. We address this with a human-in-the-loop pipeline that measures a program's coverage of an external body of knowledge, applied longitudinally to one accredited BSc in Computer Science against Computer Science Curricula 2013 (CS2013) and 2023 (CS2023). The pipeline represents the program and each guideline as structured corpora, generates candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches by semantic retrieval, and confirms them through human judgment under an explicit coverage definition. Of seven benchmarked retrievers, a reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble was strongest, and a reputed long-context model underperformed a small sentence model, so retriever choice must be measured. Both maps were validated by an independent second rater (Cohen's kappa 0.64 for CS2023, 0.69 for CS2013). The program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, near-constant across a decade. Extending the same retrieve-then-confirm design to competency articulation and cognitive depth shows that the program articulates the competency for ~88% of covered units under each guideline, yet delivers it at the recommended depth for 76% of present units under CS2023 against 95% under CS2013, a gap reflecting the newer guideline's raised expectations, not the program. The longitudinal comparison separates persistent structural gaps (parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages, systems fundamentals), uncovered against both guidelines and ABET, from differences that reflect the standard's evolution. The instrument is reusable and available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [166] [Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475)
*Thomas Bertolani,Davide Bucciarelli,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估现代扩散语言模型（DLMs），比较其在多类任务中的质量与效率，并分析推理阶段设计选择对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DLM研究在评测协议、数据集、推理预算与生成超参数上差异较大，导致难以公平比较模型能力，也难以理解其相对自回归LLM的实际优势、局限和部署权衡。

Method: 作者评估8个先进DLM在8个基准上的表现，覆盖推理、代码、翻译、知识与结构化问题求解；同时考察生成质量和计算效率，并系统分析去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小、并行解掩码策略等推理时因素。此外，还在相同训练条件下对小模型进行受控比较。

Result: 实验表明，DLM的表现显著受生成时设计选择影响；不同架构、任务类型和推理预算下呈现明显差异，并存在性能与计算效率之间的清晰权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解当代DLM的能力边界、任务适配性和部署特征提供了实践性参考，强调评估DLM时必须同时考虑质量、效率和推理配置。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized language modeling through autoregressive generation, enabling strong performance across a wide range of tasks. Recently, Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as an alternative paradigm that generates text through iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, allowing parallel refinement of entire sequences. While numerous diffusion-based architectures have been proposed, differences in evaluation protocols, datasets, inference budgets, and generation hyperparameters make it difficult to compare their capabilities and understand the trade-offs they offer. In this work, we present a systematic experimental analysis of modern DLMs. Specifically, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, translation, knowledge, and structured problem solving, while explicitly considering both generation quality and computational efficiency. Beyond downstream evaluation, we analyze the impact of key inference-time factors, including denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies, and complement large-scale experiments with controlled comparisons of smaller models trained under identical conditions. Our analysis highlights the strengths and limitations of diffusion-based language modeling across different tasks, architectures, and inference budgets. We show that the behavior of DLMs is strongly influenced by generation-time design choices, leading to distinct trade-offs between performance and computational efficiency. Overall, our study provides practical insights into the capabilities and deployment characteristics of contemporary DLMs.

</details>


### [167] [Implicit Semantic-Aware Communication Based on Hypergraph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20162)
*Yiwei Liao,Shurui Tu,Yong Xiao,Yingyu Li,Guangming Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向语义通信的超图隐式语义推理框架 HISR，用超图建模多实体高阶关系，相比传统成对图关系更具语义表达能力，并在隐式语义解释准确率上最高提升 36.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信方法常用普通图结构表示语义内容，但这类图主要捕获成对关系，难以表达现实场景中的群体交互、多实体关联和复杂关系上下文。在噪声或信道损坏条件下，这种表达不足会导致语义歧义、推理性能下降以及鲁棒性不足。

Method: 论文提出 HISR 框架，采用超图表示语义知识实体之间的高阶、多实体关系。该方法将实体及其高阶关系映射到针对不同关系上下文设计的专用语义子空间中，以解耦多样化语义交互，缓解传统图嵌入中的过平滑问题，并支持在部分信息丢失情况下进行稳健语义推理。

Result: 数值实验表明，HISR 相较于当前先进基准方法，在隐式语义解释准确率方面最高提升 36.6%。

Conclusion: HISR 通过引入超图和关系上下文感知的语义子空间，有效增强了语义通信系统对复杂高阶关系的建模能力和噪声信道下的推理鲁棒性，是对现有图语义表示方法的重要改进。

Abstract: Semantic-aware communication has emerged as a transformative paradigm for next-generation communication systems, shifting the fundamental goal from transmitting bit-level symbols to reliably recovering and understanding the semantic meaning of information. Previous studies have demonstrated that representing the semantic content of source messages as graph-based structures can significantly improve communication efficiency and the accuracy of semantic inference at the receiver. However, existing solutions typically employ graphs that capture only pairwise relationships, thereby neglecting higher-order implicit correlations commonly observed in real-world scenarios, such as group interactions, multi-entity associations, and complex relational contexts. This limitation reduces semantic expressiveness and makes semantic inference susceptible to ambiguity and performance degradation, particularly under noisy or corrupted channel conditions. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel hypergraph-based implicit semantic reasoning framework, HISR, which leverages hypergraphs to represent complex multi-entity relationships among semantic knowledge entities. In HISR, entities and their associated higher-order relations are mapped into dedicated semantic subspaces tailored to distinct relational contexts. This design not only disentangles diverse semantic interactions to mitigate the over-smoothing effects commonly found in traditional graph embedding methods but also enables robust semantic inference even when partial information loss occurs during transmission. Numerical results show that the proposed HISR achieves up to a 36.6% improvement in implicit semantic interpretation accuracy over the state-of-the-art benchmarks.

</details>


### [168] [Hidden Anchors in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19494)
*Apurba Pokharel,Ram Dantu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将多智能体大语言模型的多轮协商建模为带有隐藏内部信念“锚点”的闭环动力系统，用以解释传统共识模型无法描述的置信度越界现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体 LLM 通过交换并修正答案来提升推理与准确率，但其作用机制缺乏明确建模。传统意见动力学模型能刻画群体影响，却难以表达个体内部信念对观点持续牵引的作用。

Method: 作者提出闭环动力系统模型：每个智能体除受邻居意见影响外，还携带一个隐藏内部信念锚点。通过仅观察协商过程来恢复该锚点，并用其对留出运行的预测能力检验模型是否确实受锚点驱动。

Result: 实验表明，锚点可以从协商轨迹中恢复，并能解释智能体对正确答案的置信度超过所有初始信念凸包范围的现象。在三个开源权重模型族上，锚点驱动程度呈连续谱而非二元现象。

Conclusion: 多智能体 LLM 协商并非总能由经典共识规则充分解释；当内部锚点远离初始意见时，协商会逃离初始信念凸包，需要完整的闭环锚点模型来解释与预测。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation, where agents exchange and revise answers over several rounds, is increasingly used to improve reasoning and accuracy, yet how and why it works is rarely modelled. Such deliberation mirrors how humans reach decisions. As social animals we are pulled both by the group, the herd effect that classical opinion-dynamics models such as DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen capture, and by our own internal belief, which they do not. We model multi-agent deliberation as a closed-loop dynamical system in which each agent carries a hidden internal belief, its anchor, that continually pulls its opinion regardless of its neighbours. We show this anchor can be recovered from the deliberation alone, and that it explains a behaviour classical consensus rules forbid: an agent's confidence in the correct answer can climb past where any agent started, escaping the space (convexhull) formed by the initial beliefs. Checking whether the recovered anchor also predicts held-out runs (generalizes) gives a simple test for when a model is truly driven bysuch an anchor. Across three open-weight model families this is a spectrum, not all-or-nothing. All anchors' influence are about equally strongly, but they differ in where the anchor sits, and only when it sits far from the initial opinions does deliberation escape the hull and need the full closed-loop model.

</details>


### [169] [Thermodynamic Measure of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20231)
*Ishanu Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将智能定义为“对罕见但有效未来的合法放大”的可测量框架，并将高智能与递归自我模拟能力联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能定义往往难以统一衡量不同系统的智能水平。论文试图建立一个跨系统、可量化的智能尺度，并解释为什么具备世界模型和自我在其中的行动模拟是智能系统的重要结构。

Method: 作者从智能系统必须建模世界及自身位置的前提出发，引入递归自我模拟：系统模拟包含自身行动的未来轨迹。随后以热力学视角定义“rare-valid lift”，即系统提升罕见但符合领域约束结果概率的能力，并给出必要性与条件近充分性论证。

Result: 核心结果表明：若内部模拟不能高保真识别罕见但有效的未来，则不可能实现高 rare-valid lift；反过来，当识别保真度高且模拟中包含有效策略时，系统可实现接近受执行能力限制的最优放大效果。

Conclusion: 论文认为递归自我模拟不仅是智能的合理特征，而且在给定假设下是实现高热力学智能的必要且近乎充分条件，从而为比较被动系统、反馈控制器、语言模型、人类文本生成行为和信息引擎等提供统一度量框架。

Abstract: Can intelligence be measured? We propose that intelligence can be defined as the lawful amplification of rare but valid futures: a system increases the probability of outcomes that would be unlikely under passive dynamics but remain admissible under the constraints of the domain. We start with the premise that an intelligent system must model the world and its own place within it. Because the system is part of the world it models, this leads naturally to recursive self-simulation: the system represents futures in which its own actions are part of the trajectory. Our central results give a necessity statement and a conditional near-sufficiency statement connecting this architecture to a precise thermodynamic measure of lawful amplification of rare-valid futures: high rare-valid lift is impossible unless the internal simulation identifies rare-valid futures with high fidelity; conversely, when rare-valid fidelity is high and the simulation contains an effective policy, the achievable lift approaches the actuation-limited optimum. Thus recursive self-simulation is not merely a plausible feature of intelligence but, under the stated assumptions, is necessary and nearly sufficient for high thermodynamic intelligence. The resulting framework makes intelligence measurable on a universal scale, from passive matter and feedback controllers, large language models, and humans as text generators to Maxwell-demon-like information engines.

</details>


### [170] [DeXposure-Claw: An Agentic System for DeFi Risk Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19501)
*Aijie Shu,Bowei Chen,Wenbin Wu,Cathy Yi-Hsuan Chen,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeXposure-Claw，一个面向去中心化金融监管的、基于预测和结构化证据约束的智能体监督系统，并配套 DeXposure-Bench 评估框架，用于降低LLM误报和不当干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化金融中的信用风险传播速度快且具有网络化特征。通用LLM智能体在该场景中容易过度解读弱证据并提出高风险监管干预；现有评估也缺乏与监管目标对齐的误报/错误干预度量。

Method: 系统由三部分组成：DeXposure-FM 图时间序列基础模型预测未来风险敞口网络；确定性监测器与压力情景将预测转化为类型化警报、归因信号和情景证据；数据健康与置信度门控在升级前进行约束，最终生成可审计的监管工单及理由。同时提出六轴评估框架 DeXposure-Bench，其中决策轴使用绝对损失真值和显式错误干预率进行评分。

Result: 作者声称在五年周频真实数据上的实验完全支持该系统，表明其在预测、告警生成、证据约束和监管决策评估方面有效。

Conclusion: DeXposure-Claw 将预测模型、确定性规则、压力测试和置信门控结合，可为DeFi监管提供更可审计、误报可控、与监管目标对齐的LLM智能体框架。

Abstract: Decentralized finance exposes supervisors to fast-moving, networked credit risks. General-purpose LLM agents fit this setting poorly: they over-read weak evidence and recommend high-stakes interventions, while existing evaluations offer no regulator-aligned way to measure the resulting false alarms. We introduce DeXposure-Claw, a forecast-grounded agentic supervision system that routes LLM decisions through structured evidence: (1) DeXposure-FM, a graph time-series foundation model, forecasts future exposure networks; (2) deterministic monitors and stress scenarios then turn those forecasts into typed alerts, attribution signals, and scenario evidence; and (3) data-health and confidence gates constrain escalation before DeXposure-Claw emits auditable supervisory tickets with rationales. We further develop DeXposure-Bench, a six-axis evaluation harness, whose decision axis scores tickets against a regulator-aligned absolute-loss ground truth and an explicit false-intervention rate. Experiments on five years of weekly real data fully support our system. Code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/DeXposure-Claw.

</details>


### [171] [LLM Doesn't Know What It Doesn't Know: Detecting Epistemic Blind Spots via Cross-Model Attribution Divergence on Clinical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19509)
*Akshat Dasula,Prasanna Desikan,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在结构化临床数据预测中能否识别自身知识边界，发现其口头置信度几乎无信息价值；通过跨模型归因分歧与校准器可显著提升准确率与校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型正被用于结构化临床数据任务，但其是否能在此类任务中意识到自身不确定性尚未明确。作者希望从跨模型归因分歧角度降低结构化任务中的认知不确定性，并改善LLM可靠性评估。

Method: 比较Qwen 2.5 7B与XGBoost在预测任务中的表现，使用归因分歧分析衡量两类模型依据特征做出预测时的一致性；进一步引入少样本示例、SHAP特征证据，以及基于归因分歧信号的跨模型校准器，在不访问模型内部和不进行重复推理的前提下估计LLM可靠性。

Result: LLM口头置信度近似恒定在0.856–0.937之间，与实际准确率49%到75.3%的变化不匹配；在XGBoost高度正确时LLM准确率反而下降至64.8%，而在XGBoost中等不确定时两者准确率接近；少样本示例与SHAP证据可将ADS从1.54降至0.38，并将准确率从49%提升至75.3%；跨模型校准器将ECE从0.254降至0.080。

Conclusion: LLM在结构化数据上存在“冷启动”问题，其自报置信度不能反映真实认知不确定性；跨模型归因分歧可作为外部可靠性信号，帮助构建更具患者个体化的可靠性估计，并为实现真正的认知自我意识提供路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to structured clinical data, yet whether they can recognize the limits of their own knowledge on such tasks remains unexplored. We study this question through the lens of cross-model attribution divergence with the goal of reducing epistemic uncertainty for structured tasks, comparing Qwen 2.5 7B and XGBoost on a prediction task via attribution divergence analysis. We report four findings. First, LLM verbalized confidence is epistemically vacuous, it outputs a near-constant (0.856-0.937) regardless of whether accuracy is 49% or 75.3%, tracking prompt format rather than prediction quality. Second, the LLM exhibits an inverse difficulty effect: accuracy drops to 64.8% when XGBoost is 99% correct, but matches XGBoost (73.8% vs. 73.1%) when it is moderately uncertain. Third, few-shot examples and SHAP-derived feature evidence are orthogonal, super-additive interventions: they reduce the Attribution Disagreement Score (ADS) from 1.54 to 0.38 and improve accuracy from 49% to 75.3% without training. Fourth, a cross-model calibrator that determined LLM reliability using attribution divergence signals reduces expected calibration error from 0.254 to 0.080, replacing uninformative verbalized confidence with patient-specific reliability estimates, without accessing model internals or requiring repeated inference. We frame these findings as a cold start problem for LLMs on structured data and outline a path toward genuine epistemic self-awareness.

</details>


### [172] [REVEAL++: Differentiable Phenotypic Grouping for Vision-Language Retinal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19522)
*Ethan Elio Meidinger,Seowung Leem,Zeyun Zhao,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于视网膜图像与临床风险文本对齐的连续表型结构对比学习框架，以提升阿尔茨海默病未来发病风险预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言对齐方法虽能利用视网膜基金图像和结构化临床风险叙述预测认知衰退/AD风险，但其表型分组通常依赖离散硬聚类或固定组分配，监督信号刚性，且组形成过程与表示学习相互脱耦，难以刻画疾病风险的连续谱特征。

Method: 作者将表型相似性建模为连续、可微的权重函数，该函数由视网膜图像嵌入和风险画像嵌入的模态内相似度共同推导；这些权重通过连续聚合算子形成软多正样本关系，并引入软目标对比学习目标，使跨模态对齐与表型结构学习能够端到端联合优化。

Result: 在UK Biobank视网膜影像数据上的事件性阿尔茨海默病预测任务中，该框架持续优于离散分组式对比学习方法和标准视觉-语言基线模型。

Conclusion: 将表型相似性视为可学习的连续信号，而非固定离散分组规则，可为基于多模态视网膜与临床数据的人群级神经退行性风险建模提供更稳健、更有原则性的学习框架。

Abstract: The retina offers a noninvasive window into neurodegenerative disease, capturing subtle structural patterns associated with a risk of future cognitive decline. Vision-language alignment frameworks such as REVEAL have shown that pairing retinal fundus images with structured clinical risk narratives improves early prediction of Alzheimer's disease (AD). A key design choice in these approaches is the use of phenotypic grouping, where individuals with similar risk profiles are treated as multi-positive pairs during contrastive learning. However, existing methods operationalize phenotypic similarity as a discrete construct, relying on hard group assignments that impose rigid supervision and decouple group formation from representation learning. We propose a continuous formulation of phenotypic structure within contrastive learning. Rather than assigning samples to fixed clusters, we model inter-subject similarity as a differentiable weighting function derived from intra-modality embedding similarities in both retinal images and risk profiles. These weights define soft multi-positive relationships through a continuous aggregation operator, enabling graded supervision that reflects the spectrum nature of disease risk. We further introduce a soft-target contrastive objective that jointly learns cross-modal alignment and phenotypic structure in an end-to-end manner. Evaluated on UK Biobank retinal imaging data for incident AD prediction, the proposed framework consistently outperforms discrete group-based contrastive learning and standard vision-language baselines. By treating phenotypic similarity as a learnable, continuous signal rather than a fixed grouping rule, our approach provides a principled and robust foundation for population-scale neurodegenerative risk modeling from multi-modal retinal and clinical data.

</details>


### [173] [Emergent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19527)
*Martin Kolář*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出让大语言模型通过“良知/自省”步骤审查自身推理与输出，并结合DPO偏好优化进行在线对齐，从而减少不符合人类伦理的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究关注LLM是否能够识别自身输出与人类伦理的不一致，以及是否能在无需外部更强或更弱评判器的情况下进行自我纠正。背景是先前“Emergent Misalignment”现象表明，针对代码黑客任务微调可能诱发模型出现广泛的不伦理行为。

Method: 方法是在模型生成过程中加入一个自我审查的conscience step，让模型反思自身推理和回答；同时在训练损失中加入基于Direct Preference Optimization（DPO）的对齐项。评判信号来自模型自身的冻结副本，而非外部judge。该技术被应用于训练、微调、对抗提示和零样本场景。

Result: 实验显示，在相同的代码黑客微调场景下，一个高层次的自省式问题即可将训练方向从潜在的涌现失配引导至“涌现对齐”，使模型表现得更符合伦理约束。

Conclusion: 论文认为LLM具备一定自我识别与自我纠偏潜力；通过自省步骤和DPO对齐损失，可以构建一种不依赖外部评判器的在线对齐机制，在多种应用场景中降低不伦理输出风险。

Abstract: Can Large Language Models (LLMs) discern when their own outputs are misaligned with human ethics? And can they self-correct? We endow an LLM with a conscience step that reviews its own reasoning and outputs, and we extend the training loss with an alignment component using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to steer the model away from non-ethical outputs. The result is an online technique to align models in a wide range of applications: training, fine-tuning, adversarial prompting, and zero-shot learning. It does not require a weaker or stronger judge, relying instead on a frozen copy of itself. In previous work, the Emergent Misalignment scenario showed a range of emergent unethical behaviors from fine-tuning the model to hack code. Instead, we empirically show how to achieve Emergent Alignment: a single high-level introspective question steers training toward an ethical model under the same code hacking scenario.

</details>


### [174] [ITNet: A Learnable Integral Transform That Subsumes Convolution, Attention, and Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19538)
*Ashim Dhor,Rasel Mondal,Pin Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出积分变换网络（ITNet），用可学习的、同时依赖位置与特征的核函数统一卷积、注意力与循环类架构，并在多模态任务上达到或超过专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卷积网络、循环网络和Transformer分别体现局部性、序列记忆和内容相关交互等不同归纳偏置，长期被视为数学上彼此独立。作者希望说明这些差异并非信号处理方式的本质分裂，而是同一类可学习积分变换对象的不同参数化视角。

Method: 提出Integral Transform Network，以一个由小型MLP实现的可学习核函数建模位置与特征共同决定的成对交互；证明卷积、自注意力/多头注意力、自回归递归以及LSTM、GRU、S4、Mamba等可作为特例；并引入分块核融合、重要性加权蒙特卡洛积分和学习型低秩分解以提升可扩展性。

Result: 单一ITNet架构结合共享算子和轻量级模态特定编码器，在ImageNet-1K、GLUE、ModelNet40、VQA v2和NLVR2等任务上匹配或超过专用模型基线。

Conclusion: ITNet表明卷积、注意力和递归模型可以由统一的学习型交互机制恢复，其核心可被理解为连续算子的通用逼近框架，同时具备实践上的高效计算方案。

Abstract: Convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and transformers each encode different inductive biases -- locality, sequential memory, and content-dependent pairwise interaction -- and have remained mathematically distinct since their inception. We show that this fragmentation reflects not a fundamental diversity in how signals should be processed, but rather incomplete views of a single underlying mathematical object: a learnable integral transform. We introduce the Integral Transform Network (ITNet), a unified architecture built around a learnable kernel that depends jointly on positions and features. This kernel is implemented as a small neural network, specifically an MLP, that models pairwise interactions, enabling the model to adapt its behavior from data. We show that convolution, self-attention (including multi-head), and autoregressive recurrence (including LSTM, GRU, S4, and Mamba) arise as special cases under appropriate parameterizations, and that ITNet is a universal approximator of continuous operators. To make this practical, we develop tiled kernel fusion, importance-weighted Monte Carlo integration, and learned low-rank factorization, enabling efficient and scalable computation. A single ITNet architecture with a shared operator and lightweight modality-specific encoders matches or exceeds specialized baselines on ImageNet-1K , GLUE, ModelNet40, VQA\,v2 and NLVR2. The results demonstrate that a single learned interaction mechanism can recover the behavior of all three architectural families from data.

</details>


### [175] [Uncertainty Decomposition for Clarification Seeking in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19559)
*Gregory Matsnev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向交互式LLM智能体的提示式不确定性分解方法，将行动置信度与请求不确定性分离，使智能体能在任务描述不充分时主动澄清；在两个新增澄清基准及多个标准基准上，该方法相较既有方法显著提升澄清识别性能，并在多种LLM骨干上表现稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的偶然/认知不确定性框架难以满足交互式LLM智能体的需求，尤其无法充分支持欠规范任务中的主动澄清、可沟通不确定性表达和共享心理模型构建。同时，实际部署受到黑盒API、低延迟要求和缺乏标注轨迹的限制，使基于logprob、多次采样或训练的方法不适用，因此需要一种轻量、部署友好的提示式不确定性估计方法。

Method: 作者提出一种简单的prompt-based不确定性分解框架，区分行动置信度与请求不确定性u，其中请求不确定性用于判断任务规格是否含糊并触发澄清提问。为评估该方法，构建WebShop-Clarification和ALFWorld-Clarification两个澄清增强基准，其中50%的任务被刻意设置为欠规范；并在五个LLM骨干上，与ReAct+UE和Uncertainty-Aware Memory进行系统比较，同时还在标准WebShop、ALFWorld和REAL基准上评估故障检测能力。

Result: 在五个LLM骨干的平均结果上，该方法在ALFWorld-Clarification上的澄清F1相比ReAct+UE提升73%，相比UAM提升36%。在WebShop-Clarification上，该方法在所有骨干模型中均取得最高澄清F1；在ALFWorld-Clarification上，该方法在五个骨干中的四个上领先。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将行动置信度与请求不确定性进行提示式分解，是一种适合黑盒LLM智能体部署的有效不确定性表示方式，能够更可靠地识别欠规范任务并触发澄清行为；其收益跨多个模型骨干保持一致，说明方法具有较好的泛化性和实用部署潜力。

Abstract: Recent position papers argue that the classical aleatoric/epistemic uncertainty framework is insufficient for interactive large language model (LLM) agents and call for underspecification-aware, decomposed, and communicable uncertainty representations that can unlock new agent capabilities such as proactive clarification seeking and shared mental-model building. Practical deployment constraints -- black-box APIs, interactive latency budgets, and the absence of labeled trajectories -- rule out logprob-based, multi-sampling, and training-based methods, leaving prompt-based estimation as the most viable family for surfacing such signals at deployment time. We answer this call with a simple prompt-based decomposition that separates action confidence from request uncertainty (u), enabling the agent to ask for clarification when the task specification is ambiguous. To evaluate it, we introduce two clarification-augmented benchmarks (WebShop-Clarification and ALFWorld-Clarification) in which 50% of tasks are deliberately underspecified, and systematically compare the proposed decomposition against ReAct+UE and Uncertainty-Aware Memory (UAM) across five LLM backbones (GPT-5.1, DeepSeek-v3.2-exp, GLM-4.7, Qwen3.5-35B, GPT-OSS-120B) on these variants together with the standard WebShop, ALFWorld, and REAL benchmarks for fault detection.
